- **crosschain-bridge**: Bridge tokens to a target chain via a real signed bridge lock. The wallet signs the lock request (same payload as ``/rpc/bridge/lock``); the node creates a real BRIDGE_LOCK transaction. ``bridge_id`` equals the transfer ID and the source-chain transaction hash. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-bridge.md))
- **crosschain-bridge-status**: Check the real status of a cross-chain bridge transfer. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-bridge-status.md))
- **crosschain-confirm**: Confirm a pending bridge transfer with its real Merkle proof. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-confirm.md))
- **crosschain-pools**: Show cross-chain liquidity pools (none exist — swaps settle via bridge locks). ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-pools.md))
- **crosschain-rates**: Get configured cross-chain swap rates between chains. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-rates.md))
- **crosschain-stats**: Show cross-chain trading statistics from real persisted records. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-stats.md))
- **crosschain-status**: Get the real status of a cross-chain swap. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-status.md))
- **crosschain-swap**: Create a cross-chain token swap via a real signed bridge lock. The wallet signs the swap request; the node locks ``amount`` on the source chain and the bridge releases ``amount * rate`` on the target chain once the lock reaches finality. The reported ``from_tx_hash`` is the real hash of the on-chain BRIDGE_LOCK transaction. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-swap.md))
- **crosschain-swaps**: List persisted cross-chain swaps, optionally filtered. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/crosschain-swaps.md))
- **crosscheck**: Cross-check recent, not-yet-verified work — in ANY project.
